        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m group.outputs:[90m[39;49;00m
                producers[item].append(key)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        consumers: [96mdict[39;49;00m[[96mtuple[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m, [96mstr[39;49;00m], [96mlist[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m]] = defaultdict([96mlist[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m

        [90m# An ingredient that is BOTH belted in from the boundary and produced inside[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

        [90m# produced item, so this costs one set intersection.  `universe-matrix` is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

        [90m# property of the SAVE, not of a group.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        ceilings = _sorter_ceilings(spec)[90m[39;49;00m

    [90m    [39;49;00m[33m"""Allocate lane shards, admitting exact two-face output overflow.[39;49;00m

            [90m# key to items that actually raise the corridor demand changed 2 of 66[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# cells for +0.11%, inside the measured 12%-per-cell noise floor.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

                [94mif[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m spec.surplus_outputs [95mand[39;49;00m destinations:[90m[39;49;00m

                        [94mfor[39;49;00m destination [95min[39;49;00m destinations[90m[39;49;00m
                        [94mif[39;49;00m [95mnot[39;49;00m groups[destination].proliferated[90m[39;49;00m
                        [90m# An output lane, so the item is PRODUCED whatever the bus[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
                        [90m# also does with it: it is judged at the stack its own[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
                        [90m# sorter places.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
                        [95mand[39;49;00m surplus + _sink_demand(groups, spec, item, destination)[90m[39;49;00m
                        <= spec.lane_capacity * spec.planning_stack(item, external=[94mFalse[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
                    ][90m[39;49;00m
                    [94mif[39;49;00m shareable:[90m[39;49;00m
                        shared_boundary = [96mmin[39;49;00m([90m[39;49;00m
                            shareable,[90m[39;49;00m
                            key=[94mlambda[39;49;00m destination: ([90m[39;49;00m
                                _sink_demand(groups, spec, item, destination),[90m[39;49;00m
                                destination,[90m[39;49;00m
                            ),[90m[39;49;00m

                        item,[90m[39;49;00m

                            DEST_SEP.join((destination, [33m"[39;49;00m[33m"[39;49;00m))[90m[39;49;00m
                            [94mif[39;49;00m destination == shared_boundary[90m[39;49;00m
                            [94melse[39;49;00m destination[90m[39;49;00m

            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m group.outputs:[90m[39;49;00m
                producers[item].append(key)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        consumers: [96mdict[39;49;00m[[96mtuple[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m, [96mstr[39;49;00m], [96mlist[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m]] = defaultdict([96mlist[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m

        [90m# produced item, so this costs one set intersection.  `universe-matrix` is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

            [90m# A row's DISTANCE sizes the sorter that reaches it, and the ladder ends[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# at the Pile Sorter, so a lane whose per-machine rate exceeds what that[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# tier sustains across the row it lands on cannot be built -- no amount[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# of upgrading reaches it, and `flow.sorter_capacity` convicts every[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# packing that wires.  Judged BEFORE the split is accepted, over the[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# seating `_seat_both_fed_outermost` will actually ship, because that[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# normalisation is what moves a both-fed lane to the outermost row in[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# the first place.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            profiles = lane_reach_profiles(group.item_id, group.yaw)[90m[39;49;00m
            south_profiles = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(p [94mfor[39;49;00m p [95min[39;49;00m profiles [94mif[39;49;00m p.side == [33m"[39;49;00m[33msouth[39;49;00m[33m"[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            north_profiles = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(p [94mfor[39;49;00m p [95min[39;49;00m profiles [94mif[39;49;00m p.side == [33m"[39;49;00m[33mnorth[39;49;00m[33m"[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            sink_items = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(item [94mfor[39;49;00m item, _destination, _domain [95min[39;49;00m sinks)[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# One item on several output lanes is served by one sorter EACH, and[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# `validate._item_share` divides that item's rate between them; sizing[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# every lane at the whole rate would refuse a seating that carries it.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            lane_demand = _SeatingDemand([90m[39;49;00m
                consumed=[96mdict[39;49;00m(group.inputs),[90m[39;49;00m
                produced={[90m[39;49;00m
                    item: rate / (sink_items.count(item) [95mor[39;49;00m [94m1[39;49;00m) [94mfor[39;49;00m item, rate [95min[39;49;00m group.outputs.items()[90m[39;49;00m
                },[90m[39;49;00m
            )[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m

                        above,[90m[39;49;00m

    [33m    key.  When it cannot, ask the pose-aware slot matcher whether the minimum[39;49;00m

        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m group.outputs:[90m[39;49;00m
                producers[item].append(key)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        consumers: [96mdict[39;49;00m[[96mtuple[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m, [96mstr[39;49;00m], [96mlist[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m]] = defaultdict([96mlist[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m

    [90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# An ingredient that is BOTH belted in from the boundary and produced inside[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# the block accepts two independent feeds on one lane, so its lane head needs[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# two belt approaches.  `producers` is already keyed by every internally[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# produced item, so this costs one set intersection.  `universe-matrix` is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# the only corpus spec where it is non-empty (R4 §4); measured today it is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# exactly `{'hydrogen'}`.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        both_fed = [96mfrozenset[39;49;00m(spec.external_inputs) & [96mfrozenset[39;49;00m(producers)[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# One table for the whole spec: the tiers and their stack rows are a[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# property of the SAVE, not of a group.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        ceilings = _sorter_ceilings(spec)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m

        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m

                group.yaw,[90m[39;49;00m
                group.pitch_h,[90m[39;49;00m
            )[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# SURGICAL, not broad.  Sorting every external input first was measured[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

            [90m# reproducible +27.2% on `sequence-pair|quantum-chip|2`.  Restricting the[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# key to items that actually raise the corridor demand changed 2 of 66[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# cells for +0.11%, inside the measured 12%-per-cell noise floor.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            input_items = [96mtuple[39;49;00m([96msorted[39;49;00m(group.inputs, key=[94mlambda[39;49;00m item: (item [95mnot[39;49;00m [95min[39;49;00m both_fed, item)))[90m[39;49;00m

            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m [96msorted[39;49;00m(group.outputs):[90m[39;49;00m
                destinations = consumers.get((key, item), [])[90m[39;49;00m

    [0m[94mdef[39;49;00m[90m [39;49;00m[92m_logical_strip_plans[39;49;00m(spec: BuildSpec) -> [96mtuple[39;49;00m[_LogicalStripPlan, ...]:[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m    [39;49;00m[33m"""Allocate lane shards, admitting exact two-face output overflow.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    The historical planner budgets outputs only on the face below the machine[39;49;00m
    [33m    band.  Keep that byte-identical path whenever it can represent every cargo[39;49;00m
    [33m    key.  When it cannot, ask the pose-aware slot matcher whether the minimum[39;49;00m
    [33m    one-lane-per-cargo representation fits across both existing faces.  This is[39;49;00m
    [33m    an exact legality proof, not a widened reach: every accepted connection has[39;49;00m

            DEST_SEP,[90m[39;49;00m

            _allocate_machines,[90m[39;49;00m

                [94mreturn[39;49;00m total <= spec.lane_capacity * _input_stack(lane, spec)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
            probe = slots.probe_building(group.item_id, group.yaw)[90m[39;49;00m
            columns = [96mlen[39;49;00m(slots.attachable_columns(probe, -[94m1[39;49;00m)) [95mor[39;49;00m [94m1[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# A row's DISTANCE sizes the sorter that reaches it, and the ladder ends[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# at the Pile Sorter, so a lane whose per-machine rate exceeds what that[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# tier sustains across the row it lands on cannot be built -- no amount[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# of upgrading reaches it, and `flow.sorter_capacity` convicts every[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# packing that wires.  Judged BEFORE the split is accepted, over the[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# seating `_seat_both_fed_outermost` will actually ship, because that[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# normalisation is what moves a both-fed lane to the outermost row in[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# the first place.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            profiles = lane_reach_profiles(group.item_id, group.yaw)[90m[39;49;00m
            south_profiles = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(p [94mfor[39;49;00m p [95min[39;49;00m profiles [94mif[39;49;00m p.side == [33m"[39;49;00m[33msouth[39;49;00m[33m"[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            north_profiles = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(p [94mfor[39;49;00m p [95min[39;49;00m profiles [94mif[39;49;00m p.side == [33m"[39;49;00m[33mnorth[39;49;00m[33m"[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            sink_items = [96mtuple[39;49;00m(item [94mfor[39;49;00m item, _destination, _domain [95min[39;49;00m sinks)[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# One item on several output lanes is served by one sorter EACH, and[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# `validate._item_share` divides that item's rate between them; sizing[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# every lane at the whole rate would refuse a seating that carries it.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

                consumed=[96mdict[39;49;00m(group.inputs),[90m[39;49;00m

                },[90m[39;49;00m
            )[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m

    [0m[94mdef[39;49;00m[90m [39;49;00m[92m_logical_strip_plans[39;49;00m(spec: BuildSpec) -> [96mtuple[39;49;00m[_LogicalStripPlan, ...]:[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m    [39;49;00m[33m"""Allocate lane shards, admitting exact two-face output overflow.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    The historical planner budgets outputs only on the face below the machine[39;49;00m
    [33m    band.  Keep that byte-identical path whenever it can represent every cargo[39;49;00m
    [33m    key.  When it cannot, ask the pose-aware slot matcher whether the minimum[39;49;00m
    [33m    one-lane-per-cargo representation fits across both existing faces.  This is[39;49;00m
    [33m    an exact legality proof, not a widened reach: every accepted connection has[39;49;00m
    [33m    a prefab slot and a span no greater than ``SORTER_MAX_REACH``.[39;49;00m
    [33m    """[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m

            DEST_SEP,[90m[39;49;00m

            _allocate_machines,[90m[39;49;00m

        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m group.outputs:[90m[39;49;00m
                producers[item].append(key)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        consumers: [96mdict[39;49;00m[[96mtuple[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m, [96mstr[39;49;00m], [96mlist[39;49;00m[[96mstr[39;49;00m]] = defaultdict([96mlist[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mfor[39;49;00m item [95min[39;49;00m group.inputs:[90m[39;49;00m
                [94mfor[39;49;00m source [95min[39;49;00m producers.get(item, []):[90m[39;49;00m
                    consumers[source, item].append(key)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# An ingredient that is BOTH belted in from the boundary and produced inside[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# the block accepts two independent feeds on one lane, so its lane head needs[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# two belt approaches.  `producers` is already keyed by every internally[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# produced item, so this costs one set intersection.  `universe-matrix` is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# the only corpus spec where it is non-empty (R4 §4); measured today it is[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# exactly `{'hydrogen'}`.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        both_fed = [96mfrozenset[39;49;00m(spec.external_inputs) & [96mfrozenset[39;49;00m(producers)[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# One table for the whole spec: the tiers and their stack rows are a[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [90m# property of the SAVE, not of a group.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        ceilings = _sorter_ceilings(spec)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        plans: [96mlist[39;49;00m[_LogicalStripPlan] = [][90m[39;49;00m
        [94mfor[39;49;00m key, group [95min[39;49;00m groups.items():[90m[39;49;00m

                group.item_id,[90m[39;49;00m
                group.yaw,[90m[39;49;00m
                group.pitch_h,[90m[39;49;00m
            )[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# SURGICAL, not broad.  Sorting every external input first was measured[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# too (R4 §7, `LANEORDER=1`): same coverage, +0.78% total area and a[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# reproducible +27.2% on `sequence-pair|quantum-chip|2`.  Restricting the[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# key to items that actually raise the corridor demand changed 2 of 66[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# cells for +0.11%, inside the measured 12%-per-cell noise floor.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            input_items = [96mtuple[39;49;00m([96msorted[39;49;00m(group.inputs, key=[94mlambda[39;49;00m item: (item [95mnot[39;49;00m [95min[39;49;00m both_fed, item)))[90m[39;49;00m

    [0m[94mdef[39;49;00m[90m [39;49;00m[92m_logical_strip_plans[39;49;00m(spec: BuildSpec) -> [96mtuple[39;49;00m[_LogicalStripPlan, ...]:[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m    [39;49;00m[33m"""Allocate lane shards, admitting exact two-face output overflow.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    The historical planner budgets outputs only on the face below the machine[39;49;00m
    [33m    band.  Keep that byte-identical path whenever it can represent every cargo[39;49;00m
    [33m    key.  When it cannot, ask the pose-aware slot matcher whether the minimum[39;49;00m
    [33m    one-lane-per-cargo representation fits across both existing faces.  This is[39;49;00m
    [33m    an exact legality proof, not a widened reach: every accepted connection has[39;49;00m
